  {InternalRole : Type uInternal} {ResidualRole : Type uResidual} {OldOccurrence : Type uOld}
  {NewOccurrence : Type uNew} {ExtendedOccurrence : Type uExtended}
  {internal : RelationalFoundations.Residual.ExactInternalRealization InternalRole OldOccurrence}
  {residual : RelationalFoundations.Residual.ContractibleRole ResidualRole}
  (extension :
    RelationalFoundations.Residual.FaithfulExtension InternalRole ResidualRole OldOccurrence NewOccurrence
      ExtendedOccurrence internal residual)
  (positive : RelationalFoundations.Residual.PositiveNewPart NewOccurrence) :
  (RelationalFoundations.Residual.positiveExtension_hasUniqueResidualOccurrence extension positive).occurrence =
    positive.occurrence
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.BoundaryGenerator.{uCarrier, uExtension} (Carrier : Type uCarrier)
  (Extension : Carrier → Carrier → Type uExtension) : Type (max uCarrier uExtension)
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.BoundaryGenerator.{uCarrier, uExtension} (Carrier : Type uCarrier)
  (Extension : Carrier → Carrier → Type uExtension) : Type (max uCarrier uExtension)
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.BoundaryGenerator.continuation_ne_boundary.{uCarrier, uExtension}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Extension : Carrier → Carrier → Type uExtension}
  (generator : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.BoundaryGenerator Carrier Extension) :
  generator.continuation ≠ generator.boundary
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.BoundaryGenerator.continuation_ne_boundary.{uCarrier, uExtension}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Extension : Carrier → Carrier → Type uExtension}

  generator.continuation ≠ generator.boundary

RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit.mapFaithful_id.{uCarrier, uRegime, uInterpretation}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Faithful : Carrier → Type uInterpretation} {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime}
  (exit : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit Faithful Regime) :
  (exit.mapFaithful fun x faithful => faithful) = exit
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit.mapFaithful_id.{uCarrier, uRegime, uInterpretation}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Faithful : Carrier → Type uInterpretation} {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime}
  (exit : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit Faithful Regime) :
  (exit.mapFaithful fun x faithful => faithful) = exit
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit.mapFaithful_comp.{uCarrier, uRegime, uInterpretation, uFaithful',
    uFaithful''}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Faithful : Carrier → Type uInterpretation} {Faithful' : Carrier → Type uFaithful'}
  {Faithful'' : Carrier → Type uFaithful''} {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime}
  (exit : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit Faithful Regime)
  (first : (candidate : Carrier) → Faithful candidate → Faithful' candidate)
  (second : (candidate : Carrier) → Faithful' candidate → Faithful'' candidate) :
  (exit.mapFaithful first).mapFaithful second =
    exit.mapFaithful fun candidate faithful => second candidate (first candidate faithful)
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit.mapFaithful_comp.{uCarrier, uRegime, uInterpretation, uFaithful',
    uFaithful''}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Faithful : Carrier → Type uInterpretation} {Faithful' : Carrier → Type uFaithful'}
  {Faithful'' : Carrier → Type uFaithful''} {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime}
  (exit : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit Faithful Regime)
  (first : (candidate : Carrier) → Faithful candidate → Faithful' candidate)
  (second : (candidate : Carrier) → Faithful' candidate → Faithful'' candidate) :
  (exit.mapFaithful first).mapFaithful second =
    exit.mapFaithful fun candidate faithful => second candidate (first candidate faithful)
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit.restrictRegime_id.{uCarrier, uRegime, uInterpretation}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Faithful : Carrier → Type uInterpretation} {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime}
  (exit : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit Faithful Regime) :
  (exit.restrictRegime fun x regime => regime) = exit
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit.restrictRegime_id.{uCarrier, uRegime, uInterpretation}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Faithful : Carrier → Type uInterpretation} {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime}
  (exit : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit Faithful Regime) :
  (exit.restrictRegime fun x regime => regime) = exit
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit.restrictRegime_comp.{uCarrier, uRegime, uInterpretation, uRegime',
    uRegime''}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Faithful : Carrier → Type uInterpretation} {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime}
  {Regime' : Carrier → Type uRegime'} {Regime'' : Carrier → Type uRegime''}
  (exit : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit Faithful Regime)
  (first : (candidate : Carrier) → Regime' candidate → Regime candidate)
  (second : (candidate : Carrier) → Regime'' candidate → Regime' candidate) :
  (exit.restrictRegime first).restrictRegime second =
    exit.restrictRegime fun candidate regime => first candidate (second candidate regime)
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit.restrictRegime_comp.{uCarrier, uRegime, uInterpretation, uRegime',
    uRegime''}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Faithful : Carrier → Type uInterpretation} {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime}
  {Regime' : Carrier → Type uRegime'} {Regime'' : Carrier → Type uRegime''}
  (exit : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit Faithful Regime)
  (first : (candidate : Carrier) → Regime' candidate → Regime candidate)
  (second : (candidate : Carrier) → Regime'' candidate → Regime' candidate) :
  (exit.restrictRegime first).restrictRegime second =
    exit.restrictRegime fun candidate regime => first candidate (second candidate regime)
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit.mapFaithful_restrictRegime.{uCarrier, uRegime, uInterpretation, uFaithful',
    uRegime'}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Faithful : Carrier → Type uInterpretation} {Faithful' : Carrier → Type uFaithful'}
  {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime} {Regime' : Carrier → Type uRegime'}
  (exit : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit Faithful Regime)
  (map : (candidate : Carrier) → Faithful candidate → Faithful' candidate)
  (restrict : (candidate : Carrier) → Regime' candidate → Regime candidate) :
  (exit.mapFaithful map).restrictRegime restrict = (exit.restrictRegime restrict).mapFaithful map
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit.mapFaithful_restrictRegime.{uCarrier, uRegime, uInterpretation, uFaithful',
    uRegime'}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Faithful : Carrier → Type uInterpretation} {Faithful' : Carrier → Type uFaithful'}
  {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime} {Regime' : Carrier → Type uRegime'}
  (exit : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.RegimeExit Faithful Regime)
  (map : (candidate : Carrier) → Faithful candidate → Faithful' candidate)
  (restrict : (candidate : Carrier) → Regime' candidate → Regime candidate) :
  (exit.mapFaithful map).restrictRegime restrict = (exit.restrictRegime restrict).mapFaithful map

RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.UniformRegimeExit.reindexImplementation.{uCarrier, uRegime, uInterpretation,
    uImplementation, uImplementation'}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Implementation : Type uImplementation} {Implementation' : Type uImplementation'}
  {Faithful : Implementation → Carrier → Type uInterpretation} {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime}
  (exit : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.UniformRegimeExit Implementation Faithful Regime)
  (reindex : Implementation' → Implementation) :
  RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.UniformRegimeExit Implementation'
    (fun implementation' candidate => Faithful (reindex implementation') candidate) Regime
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.UniformRegimeExit.reindexImplementation.{uCarrier, uRegime, uInterpretation,
    uImplementation, uImplementation'}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Implementation : Type uImplementation} {Implementation' : Type uImplementation'}
  {Faithful : Implementation → Carrier → Type uInterpretation} {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime}
  (exit : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.UniformRegimeExit Implementation Faithful Regime)
  (reindex : Implementation' → Implementation) :
  RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.UniformRegimeExit Implementation'
    (fun implementation' candidate => Faithful (reindex implementation') candidate) Regime
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.UniformRegimeExit.reindex_id.{uCarrier, uRegime, uInterpretation, uImplementation}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Implementation : Type uImplementation}
  {Faithful : Implementation → Carrier → Type uInterpretation} {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime}
  (exit : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.UniformRegimeExit Implementation Faithful Regime) :
  exit.reindexImplementation id = exit

  {Faithful : Implementation → Carrier → Type uInterpretation} {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime}

RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.UniformRegimeExit.reindex_comp.{uCarrier, uRegime, uInterpretation, uImplementation,
    uImplementation', uImplementation''}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Implementation : Type uImplementation} {Implementation' : Type uImplementation'}
  {Implementation'' : Type uImplementation''} {Faithful : Implementation → Carrier → Type uInterpretation}
  {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime}
  (exit : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.UniformRegimeExit Implementation Faithful Regime)
  (first : Implementation' → Implementation) (second : Implementation'' → Implementation') :
  (exit.reindexImplementation first).reindexImplementation second = exit.reindexImplementation (first ∘ second)
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.UniformRegimeExit.reindex_comp.{uCarrier, uRegime, uInterpretation, uImplementation,
    uImplementation', uImplementation''}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Implementation : Type uImplementation} {Implementation' : Type uImplementation'}
  {Implementation'' : Type uImplementation''} {Faithful : Implementation → Carrier → Type uInterpretation}
  {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime}
  (exit : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.UniformRegimeExit Implementation Faithful Regime)
  (first : Implementation' → Implementation) (second : Implementation'' → Implementation') :
  (exit.reindexImplementation first).reindexImplementation second = exit.reindexImplementation (first ∘ second)
RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.UniformRegimeExit.reindex_at.{uCarrier, uRegime, uInterpretation, uImplementation,
    uImplementation'}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Implementation : Type uImplementation} {Implementation' : Type uImplementation'}
  {Faithful : Implementation → Carrier → Type uInterpretation} {Regime : Carrier → Type uRegime}
  (exit : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.UniformRegimeExit Implementation Faithful Regime)
  (reindex : Implementation' → Implementation) (implementation : Implementation') :
  (exit.reindexImplementation reindex).atImplementation implementation = exit.atImplementation (reindex implementation)

RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.CoreCoupledObstructedRegime.{uCarrier, uExtension, uInternal, uResidual, uNew,
    uRegime, uInterpretation, uAttempt, uContext}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Extension : Carrier → Carrier → Type uExtension}
  (generator : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.BoundaryGenerator Carrier Extension)
  (Context : Carrier → Type uContext) (NewOccurrence : {candidate : Carrier} → Context candidate → Type uNew)
  (InternalRole : Type uInternal) (ResidualRole : Type uResidual)
  (residual : RelationalFoundations.Residual.ContractibleRole ResidualRole) :
  Type
    (max
        (max
            (max
                (max (max (max (max (max (uAttempt + 1) uCarrier) uContext) uExtension) uInternal)
                    (uInterpretation + 1))
                uNew)
            (uRegime + 1))
        uResidual)

RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.CoupledObstructedRegime.{uCarrier, uExtension, uInternal, uResidual, uOld, uNew,
    uCombined, uRegime, uInterpretation, uAttempt, uContext}
  {Carrier : Type uCarrier} {Extension : Carrier → Carrier → Type uExtension}
  (generator : RelationalFoundations.Diagnostics.BoundaryGenerator Carrier Extension)
  (Context : Carrier → Type uContext) (NewOccurrence : {candidate : Carrier} → Context candidate → Type uNew)
  (CombinedOccurrence : {candidate : Carrier} → Context candidate → Type uCombined) (InternalRole : Type uInternal)
  (ResidualRole : Type uResidual) (OldOccurrence : Type uOld)
  (internal : RelationalFoundations.Residual.ExactInternalRealization InternalRole OldOccurrence)
  (residual : RelationalFoundations.Residual.ContractibleRole ResidualRole) :
  Type
    (max
        (max
            (max
                (max
                    (max (max (max (max (max (max (uAttempt + 1) uCarrier) uCombined) uContext) uExtension) uInternal)
                        (uInterpretation + 1))
                    uNew)
                uOld)
            (uRegime + 1))
        uResidual)

RelationalFoundations.History.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} (Step : State → State → Type uB) :
  State → State → Type (max uA uB)
RelationalFoundations.History.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} (Step : State → State → Type uB) :
  State → State → Type (max uA uB)
RelationalFoundations.History.append.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB} {a b c : State} :
  RelationalFoundations.History Step a b →
    RelationalFoundations.History Step b c → RelationalFoundations.History Step a c
RelationalFoundations.History.append.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB} {a b c : State} :
  RelationalFoundations.History Step a b →
    RelationalFoundations.History Step b c → RelationalFoundations.History Step a c
RelationalFoundations.History.appendRootExact.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB} {a b : State}
  (history : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b) : history.append RelationalFoundations.History.root = history
RelationalFoundations.History.appendRootExact.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB} {a b : State}
  (history : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b) : history.append RelationalFoundations.History.root = history
RelationalFoundations.History.appendExtendExact.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB}
  {a b c d : State} (firstHistory : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b)
  (continuation : RelationalFoundations.History Step b c) (step : Step c d) :
  firstHistory.append (continuation.extend step) = (firstHistory.append continuation).extend step
RelationalFoundations.History.appendExtendExact.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB}
  {a b c d : State} (firstHistory : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b)
  (continuation : RelationalFoundations.History Step b c) (step : Step c d) :
  firstHistory.append (continuation.extend step) = (firstHistory.append continuation).extend step
RelationalFoundations.History.append_root.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB} {a b : State}
  (history : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b) : history.append RelationalFoundations.History.root = history
RelationalFoundations.History.append_root.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB} {a b : State}
  (history : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b) : history.append RelationalFoundations.History.root = history
RelationalFoundations.History.root_append.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB} {a b : State}
  (history : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b) : RelationalFoundations.History.root.append history = history
RelationalFoundations.History.root_append.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB} {a b : State}
  (history : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b) : RelationalFoundations.History.root.append history = history
RelationalFoundations.History.append_associative.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB}
  {a b c d : State} (first : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b) (second : RelationalFoundations.History Step b c)
  (third : RelationalFoundations.History Step c d) :
  (first.append second).append third = first.append (second.append third)
RelationalFoundations.History.append_associative.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB}
  {a b c d : State} (first : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b) (second : RelationalFoundations.History Step b c)
  (third : RelationalFoundations.History Step c d) :
  (first.append second).append third = first.append (second.append third)

  RelationalFoundations.History Step a b → Type (max uA uB)

  {source target : State} (history : RelationalFoundations.History Step source target) (Value : Type uV) :
  Type (max (max uA uB) uV)

  (precedes : RelationalFoundations.History.OccurrencePrecedes first second) : first ≠ second

  {a b : State} {history : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b} {first second : history.Occurrence}
  (precedes : RelationalFoundations.History.OccurrencePrecedes first second) : first ≠ second
RelationalFoundations.History.OccurrencePrecedes.trichotomy.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB}
  {a b : State} {history : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b} (first second : history.Occurrence) :
  first = second ∨
    RelationalFoundations.History.OccurrencePrecedes first second ∨
      RelationalFoundations.History.OccurrencePrecedes second first
RelationalFoundations.History.OccurrencePrecedes.trichotomy.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB}
  {a b : State} {history : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b} (first second : history.Occurrence) :
  first = second ∨
    RelationalFoundations.History.OccurrencePrecedes first second ∨
      RelationalFoundations.History.OccurrencePrecedes second first

  {a b c : State} {firstHistory : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b} (oldOccurrence : firstHistory.Occurrence)
  {continuation : RelationalFoundations.History Step b c} (newOccurrence : continuation.Occurrence) :
  RelationalFoundations.History.embedLeftOccurrence oldOccurrence continuation ≠
    firstHistory.embedRightOccurrence newOccurrence

  {a b c : State} {firstHistory : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b} (oldOccurrence : firstHistory.Occurrence)

  RelationalFoundations.History.embedLeftOccurrence oldOccurrence continuation ≠
    firstHistory.embedRightOccurrence newOccurrence

  {a b c : State} {firstHistory : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b}
  {continuation : RelationalFoundations.History Step b c} :

RelationalFoundations.History.classifyAppendOccurrence.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB}
  {a b c : State} (firstHistory : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b)
  (continuation : RelationalFoundations.History Step b c) (occurrence : (firstHistory.append continuation).Occurrence) :
  (old : firstHistory.Occurrence) ×
      PLift (RelationalFoundations.History.embedLeftOccurrence old continuation = occurrence) ⊕
    (new : continuation.Occurrence) × PLift (firstHistory.embedRightOccurrence new = occurrence)
RelationalFoundations.History.classifyAppendOccurrence.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB}
  {a b c : State} (firstHistory : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b)
  (continuation : RelationalFoundations.History Step b c) (occurrence : (firstHistory.append continuation).Occurrence) :
  (old : firstHistory.Occurrence) ×
      PLift (RelationalFoundations.History.embedLeftOccurrence old continuation = occurrence) ⊕
    (new : continuation.Occurrence) × PLift (firstHistory.embedRightOccurrence new = occurrence)
RelationalFoundations.History.embedRightOccurrence_injective.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB}
  {a b c : State} (firstHistory : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b)
  {continuation : RelationalFoundations.History Step b c} : Function.Injective firstHistory.embedRightOccurrence

  {a b c : State} (firstHistory : RelationalFoundations.History Step a b)
  {continuation : RelationalFoundations.History Step b c} : Function.Injective firstHistory.embedRightOccurrence

RelationalFoundations.History.rootOrPositive.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB}
  {source target : State} (history : RelationalFoundations.History Step source target) :
  PLift (target = source) × PLift (history ≍ RelationalFoundations.History.root) ⊕
    (positive : RelationalFoundations.History.Positive Step source target) × PLift (history = positive.toHistory)
RelationalFoundations.History.rootOrPositive.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB}
  {source target : State} (history : RelationalFoundations.History Step source target) :
  PLift (target = source) × PLift (history ≍ RelationalFoundations.History.root) ⊕
    (positive : RelationalFoundations.History.Positive Step source target) × PLift (history = positive.toHistory)
RelationalFoundations.History.appendRootOrPositive.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB}
  {source middle target : State} (firstHistory : RelationalFoundations.History Step source middle)
  (continuation : RelationalFoundations.History Step middle target) :
  PLift (target = middle) × PLift (firstHistory.append continuation ≍ firstHistory) ⊕
    (positive : RelationalFoundations.History.Positive Step middle target) × PLift (continuation = positive.toHistory)
RelationalFoundations.History.appendRootOrPositive.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB}
  {source middle target : State} (firstHistory : RelationalFoundations.History Step source middle)
  (continuation : RelationalFoundations.History Step middle target) :
  PLift (target = middle) × PLift (firstHistory.append continuation ≍ firstHistory) ⊕
    (positive : RelationalFoundations.History.Positive Step middle target) × PLift (continuation = positive.toHistory)

StrongPerimetralTurning.classifyDeploymentOccurrence.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB}
  {source middle target : State} {firstStep : Step source middle}
  (continuation : RelationalFoundations.History Step middle target)
  (occurrence : ((RelationalFoundations.History.root.extend firstStep).append continuation).Occurrence) :
  PLift
      (occurrence =
        RelationalFoundations.History.embedLeftOccurrence RelationalFoundations.History.Occurrence.last continuation) ⊕
    (later : continuation.Occurrence) ×
      PLift (occurrence = (RelationalFoundations.History.root.extend firstStep).embedRightOccurrence later)
StrongPerimetralTurning.classifyDeploymentOccurrence.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA} {Step : State → State → Type uB}
  {source middle target : State} {firstStep : Step source middle}
  (continuation : RelationalFoundations.History Step middle target)
  (occurrence : ((RelationalFoundations.History.root.extend firstStep).append continuation).Occurrence) :
  PLift
      (occurrence =
        RelationalFoundations.History.embedLeftOccurrence RelationalFoundations.History.Occurrence.last continuation) ⊕
    (later : continuation.Occurrence) ×
      PLift (occurrence = (RelationalFoundations.History.root.extend firstStep).embedRightOccurrence later)

    StrongPerimetralTurning.boundaryDifferenceReadout
      (StrongPerimetralTurning.perimeterPositionSource P position).snd.snd

StrongPerimetralTurning.History.factorInitialGeneratedStep.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4, u_5, u_6, u_7}
  {P : StrongPerimetralTurning.CircularPresentation}
  {source middle target : StrongPerimetralTurning.PositiveConstitution P}
  (history : StrongPerimetralTurning.GeneratedHistory source target)
  (step : StrongPerimetralTurning.GeneratedStep source middle)
  (occurrence : RelationalFoundations.History.Occurrence history)
  (locatedStepExact : occurrence.locatedStep = { source := source, target := middle, step := step }) :
  (continuation : StrongPerimetralTurning.GeneratedHistory middle target) ×
    { recompose //
      StrongPerimetralTurning.transportOccurrence recompose
          (RelationalFoundations.History.embedLeftOccurrence RelationalFoundations.History.Occurrence.last
            continuation) =
        occurrence }

        occurrence }
StrongPerimetralTurning.History.extractRightOccurrenceAfterSingle.{uA, uB} {State : Type uA}
  {Step : State → State → Type uB} {source middle target : State} (firstStep : Step source middle)
  (continuation : RelationalFoundations.History Step middle target)
  {history : RelationalFoundations.History Step source target}
  (recompose : (RelationalFoundations.History.root.extend firstStep).append continuation = history)
  (occurrence : history.Occurrence)
  (notFirst :
    StrongPerimetralTurning.transportOccurrence recompose
        (RelationalFoundations.History.embedLeftOccurrence RelationalFoundations.History.Occurrence.last continuation) ≠
      occurrence) :
  (newOccurrence : continuation.Occurrence) ×
    PLift
      (StrongPerimetralTurning.transportOccurrence recompose
          ((RelationalFoundations.History.root.extend firstStep).embedRightOccurrence newOccurrence) =
        occurrence)

  (P : StrongPerimetralTurning.CircularPresentation)
  {node : StrongPerimetralTurning.LocalNode P.Explicit P.Implicit P.Compatible P.Difference P.Provenance}

  (difference : StrongPerimetralTurning.BoundaryDifference P data)
  (target : StrongPerimetralTurning.PositiveConstitution P)
  (history :
    StrongPerimetralTurning.GeneratedHistory (StrongPerimetralTurning.positiveAtRemaining P remaining data difference)
      target)
  (realize : StrongPerimetralTurning.NonClosingPosition remaining → RelationalFoundations.History.Occurrence history) :
  Function.Injective realize →
    (∀ (position : StrongPerimetralTurning.NonClosingPosition remaining),
        (realize position).locatedStep = StrongPerimetralTurning.positionLocatedStep P data difference position) →
      (continuation :
        StrongPerimetralTurning.GeneratedHistory
          (StrongPerimetralTurning.deployRemaining P remaining data difference).fst target) ×
        PLift
          (RelationalFoundations.History.append
              (StrongPerimetralTurning.deployRemaining P remaining data difference).snd continuation =
            history)

  {source middle target : State} (firstHistory : RelationalFoundations.History Step source middle)
  (continuation : RelationalFoundations.History Step middle target) :
  PLift ((firstHistory.append continuation).length = firstHistory.length + continuation.length)
